import { Body,Controller,Param,Post,Req,UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClinicalAuthenticationGuard } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-authentication.guard';
import { ClinicalRequestContextService } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-request-context.service';
import { NormalizationService } from './normalization.service';
import { CanonicalObservationDto,CompleteNormalizationDto,CreateCanonicalDatasetDto,StartNormalizationDto,WaveformSeriesDto,WaveformChannelDto,ImagingStudyDto,ImagingSeriesDto,ImagingArtifactRefDto,MediaRecordingDto,DatasetLifecycleDto } from './normalization.dto';
@UseGuards(ClinicalAuthenticationGuard) @Controller('clinical/normalization')
export class NormalizationController{
 constructor(private readonly s:NormalizationService,private readonly c:ClinicalRequestContextService){}
 @Post('runs') start(@Body() d:StartNormalizationDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.start(d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('runs/:id/complete') complete(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CompleteNormalizationDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.complete(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('runs/:id/datasets') dataset(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CreateCanonicalDatasetDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.createDataset(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('datasets/:id/observations') obs(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CanonicalObservationDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addObservation(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('datasets/:id/waveforms') wf(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:WaveformSeriesDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addWaveformSeries(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('waveforms/:id/channels') wfc(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:WaveformChannelDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addWaveformChannel(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('datasets/:id/imaging-studies') img(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:ImagingStudyDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addImagingStudy(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('imaging-studies/:id/series') imgs(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:ImagingSeriesDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addImagingSeries(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('imaging-series/:id/artifacts') imga(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:ImagingArtifactRefDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.linkImagingArtifact(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('datasets/:id/media-recordings') media(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:MediaRecordingDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addMediaRecording(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('datasets/:id/lifecycle') life(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:DatasetLifecycleDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.lifecycle(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}

}

import { Body,Controller,Param,Post,Req,UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClinicalAuthenticationGuard } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-authentication.guard';
import { ClinicalRequestContextService } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-request-context.service';
import { NormalizationService } from './normalization.service';
import { CanonicalObservationDto,CompleteNormalizationDto,CreateCanonicalDatasetDto,StartNormalizationDto,WaveformSeriesDto,WaveformChannelDto,ImagingStudyDto,ImagingSeriesDto,ImagingArtifactRefDto,MediaRecordingDto,DatasetLifecycleDto } from './normalization.dto';
@UseGuards(ClinicalAuthenticationGuard) @Controller('clinical/normalization')
export class NormalizationController{
 constructor(private readonly s:NormalizationService,private readonly c:ClinicalRequestContextService){}
 @Post('runs') start(@Body() d:StartNormalizationDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.start(d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('runs/:id/complete') complete(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CompleteNormalizationDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.complete(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('runs/:id/datasets') dataset(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CreateCanonicalDatasetDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.createDataset(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('datasets/:id/observations') obs(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CanonicalObservationDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addObservation(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('datasets/:id/waveforms') wf(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:WaveformSeriesDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addWaveformSeries(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('waveforms/:id/channels') wfc(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:WaveformChannelDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addWaveformChannel(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('datasets/:id/imaging-studies') img(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:ImagingStudyDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addImagingStudy(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('imaging-studies/:id/series') imgs(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:ImagingSeriesDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addImagingSeries(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('imaging-series/:id/artifacts') imga(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:ImagingArtifactRefDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.linkImagingArtifact(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('datasets/:id/media-recordings') media(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:MediaRecordingDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addMediaRecording(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('datasets/:id/lifecycle') life(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:DatasetLifecycleDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.lifecycle(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}

FILE: C:\realtime-server\PHASE2_COMPLETE_IMPLEMENTATION\PHASE2_COMPLETE_IMPLEMENTATION\src\devices\normalization\normalization.dto.ts
============================================================
import { IsIn,IsInt,IsObject,IsOptional,IsString,IsUUID,Min } from '../../clinical-common/dto';
export class StartNormalizationDto {
 @IsUUID() acquisitionSessionId!:string; @IsUUID() normalizationProfileId!:string;
 @IsString() normalizerEngineName!:string; @IsString() normalizerEngineVersion!:string;
 @IsString() normalizerBuildSha256!:string; @IsString() triggeredByComponent!:string;
 @IsOptional() sourceArtifactIds?:string[];
}
export class CompleteNormalizationDto {
 @IsIn(['VALIDATED','REVIEW_REQUIRED','QUARANTINED','FAILED']) runStatus!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() inputFingerprintSha256?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() outputFingerprintSha256?:string;
}
export class CreateCanonicalDatasetDto {
 @IsIn(['VITAL_SIGNS','ECG_WAVEFORM','IMAGING_STUDY','ULTRASOUND_STUDY','POC_LAB_RESULT','AUSCULTATION_AUDIO','FUNCTIONAL_TEST','OTHER']) datasetKind!:string;
 @IsString() canonicalSchemaVersion!:string;
 @IsIn(['VALIDATED','REVIEW_REQUIRED','QUARANTINED','SUPERSEDED','VOID']) datasetStatus!:string;
 @IsInt() @Min(1) versionNo!:number; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() supersedesDatasetId?:string;
 @IsString() sourceFingerprintSha256!:string; @IsString() canonicalFingerprintSha256!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() effectiveStartAt?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() effectiveEndAt?:string;
 @IsOptional() reviewReasonCodes?:string[];
}
export class CanonicalObservationDto {
 @IsUUID() canonicalConceptId!:string; @IsIn(['QUANTITY','TEXT','CODE','BOOLEAN']) valueType!:string;
 @IsOptional() valueNumeric?:number; @IsOptional() @IsString() valueText?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() valueCode?:string;
 @IsOptional() valueBoolean?:boolean; @IsOptional() @IsString() unitUcum?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() measuredAt?:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() sourceArtifactId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() sourceObservationCode?:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() sourceUnitCode?:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() normalizationRuleId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() mappingRuleSha256?:string;
}

export class WaveformSeriesDto {
 @IsUUID() waveformConceptId!:string; @IsUUID() sourceArtifactId!:string;
 sampleRateHz!:number; @IsInt() @Min(1) channelCount!:number; @IsOptional() @IsInt() @Min(0) durationMs?:number;
 @IsString() sampleEncoding!:string; @IsString() rawStorageRef!:string;
}
export class WaveformChannelDto {
 @IsInt() @Min(0) channelIndex!:number; @IsString() canonicalLeadCode!:string; @IsString() sourceLabel!:string;
 @IsString() physicalUnitUcum!:string; gainToPhysicalUnit!:number; @IsOptional() offsetToPhysicalUnit?:number;
}
export class ImagingStudyDto {
 @IsUUID() imagingConceptId!:string; @IsString() studyInstanceUid!:string; @IsString() modality!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() bodySiteConceptId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() acquisitionProtocolCode?:string;
}
export class ImagingSeriesDto {
 @IsString() seriesInstanceUid!:string; @IsString() modality!:string; @IsOptional() @IsInt() seriesNumber?:number;
}
export class ImagingArtifactRefDto {
 @IsUUID() artifactId!:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() sopInstanceUid?:string;
}
export class MediaRecordingDto {
 @IsUUID() mediaConceptId!:string; @IsUUID() sourceArtifactId!:string; @IsIn(['AUDIO','VIDEO']) mediaType!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() bodySiteConceptId?:string; @IsOptional() sampleRateHz?:number; @IsOptional() @IsInt() @Min(1) channelCount?:number;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() codec?:string; @IsOptional() @IsInt() @Min(0) durationMs?:number; @IsString() rawStorageRef!:string;
}
export class DatasetLifecycleDto {
 @IsIn(['REVIEW_REQUIRED','QUARANTINED','SUPERSEDED','VOIDED']) eventType!:string; @IsString() reasonCode!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() replacementDatasetId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() actorComponent?:string;
}

FILE: C:\realtime-server\PHASE2_COMPLETE_IMPLEMENTATION\PHASE2_COMPLETE_IMPLEMENTATION\src\devices\normalization\normalization.service.ts
============================================================
import { ConflictException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { ClinicalRequestContext } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-types';
import { ClinicalCommandExecutor } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-command.executor';
import { ClinicalTableRepository } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-table.repository';

@Injectable()
export class NormalizationService {
 constructor(private readonly tables:ClinicalTableRepository,private readonly commands:ClinicalCommandExecutor){}

 start(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'NORMALIZATION_START',resourceType:'ACQUISITION',resourceId:d.acquisitionSessionId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const profile:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_normalization_profiles',d.normalizationProfileId,client);
    if(profile.profile_status!=='APPROVED') throw new ConflictException('Normalization profile must be APPROVED');

    const artifacts:any[]=d.sourceArtifactIds?.length
      ? await Promise.all(d.sourceArtifactIds.map((id:string)=>this.tables.findById('clinical_artifacts',id,client)))
      : await this.tables.list('clinical_artifacts',{acquisition_session_id:d.acquisitionSessionId},200,client);

    if(!artifacts.length) throw new ConflictException('No source artifacts');
    for(const a of artifacts){
      if(a.acquisition_session_id!==d.acquisitionSessionId) throw new ConflictException('Artifact acquisition mismatch');
      if(a.artifact_status!=='INTEGRITY_VERIFIED' || a.integrity_status!=='VERIFIED'){
        throw new ConflictException(`Artifact ${a.id} is not integrity verified`);
      }
      if(!a.content_sha256) throw new ConflictException(`Artifact ${a.id} has no content SHA-256`);
    }

    const run:any=await this.tables.insert('clinical_normalization_runs',{
      acquisition_session_id:d.acquisitionSessionId,normalization_profile_id:d.normalizationProfileId,
      run_status:'STARTED',normalizer_engine_name:d.normalizerEngineName,normalizer_engine_version:d.normalizerEngineVersion,
      normalizer_build_sha256:d.normalizerBuildSha256,triggered_by_user_id:ctx.userId,triggered_by_component:d.triggeredByComponent,
    },client);

    for(const a of artifacts){
      await this.tables.insert('clinical_normalization_run_sources',{
        normalization_run_id:run.id,artifact_id:a.id,source_artifact_sha256:a.content_sha256,
        source_artifact_status:a.artifact_status,source_storage_ref:a.storage_ref,
      },client);
    }
    return run;
   }});
 }

 complete(runId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'NORMALIZATION_COMPLETE',resourceType:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',resourceId:runId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const run:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_normalization_runs',runId,client,true);
    if(!['STARTED','VALIDATING'].includes(run.run_status)) throw new ConflictException('Normalization run already terminal');
    return this.tables.updateById('clinical_normalization_runs',runId,{
      run_status:d.runStatus,input_fingerprint_sha256:d.inputFingerprintSha256,
      output_fingerprint_sha256:d.outputFingerprintSha256,completed_at:new Date(),
    },client);
   }});
 }

 createDataset(runId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_DATASET_CREATE',resourceType:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',resourceId:runId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const run:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_normalization_runs',runId,client);
    if(!['VALIDATED','REVIEW_REQUIRED','QUARANTINED'].includes(run.run_status)) throw new ConflictException('Run is not ready to publish a dataset');
    const dataset:any=await this.tables.insert('canonical_clinical_datasets',{
      dataset_number:`CDS-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,acquisition_session_id:run.acquisition_session_id,
      normalization_run_id:runId,normalization_profile_id:run.normalization_profile_id,dataset_kind:d.datasetKind,
      canonical_schema_version:d.canonicalSchemaVersion,dataset_status:d.datasetStatus,version_no:d.versionNo,
      supersedes_dataset_id:d.supersedesDatasetId,source_fingerprint_sha256:d.sourceFingerprintSha256,
      canonical_fingerprint_sha256:d.canonicalFingerprintSha256,effective_start_at:d.effectiveStartAt?new Date(d.effectiveStartAt):undefined,
      effective_end_at:d.effectiveEndAt?new Date(d.effectiveEndAt):undefined,normalized_at:new Date(),review_reason_codes:d.reviewReasonCodes??[],
    },client);
    const sources:any[]=await this.tables.list('clinical_normalization_run_sources',{normalization_run_id:runId},200,client);
    for(const s of sources){
      await this.tables.insert('canonical_dataset_source_artifacts',{dataset_id:dataset.id,artifact_id:s.artifact_id,artifact_sha256:s.source_artifact_sha256},client);
    }
    return dataset;
   }});
 }

 addObservation(datasetId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_OBSERVATION_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:datasetId,
   handler:client=>this.tables.insert('canonical_observations',{
    dataset_id:datasetId,canonical_concept_id:d.canonicalConceptId,value_type:d.valueType,value_numeric:d.valueNumeric,
    value_text:d.valueText,value_code:d.valueCode,value_boolean:d.valueBoolean,unit_ucum:d.unitUcum,
    measured_at:d.measuredAt?new Date(d.measuredAt):undefined,source_artifact_id:d.sourceArtifactId,
    source_observation_code:d.sourceObservationCode,source_unit_code:d.sourceUnitCode,
    normalization_rule_id:d.normalizationRuleId,mapping_rule_sha256:d.mappingRuleSha256,
   },client)});
 }
 addWaveformSeries(datasetId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_WAVEFORM_SERIES_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:datasetId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const dataset:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_clinical_datasets',datasetId,client);
    if(!['VALIDATED','REVIEW_REQUIRED'].includes(dataset.dataset_status)) throw new ConflictException('Dataset is not publishable');
    const artifact:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_artifacts',d.sourceArtifactId,client);
    if(artifact.acquisition_session_id!==dataset.acquisition_session_id || artifact.integrity_status!=='VERIFIED') throw new ConflictException('Waveform source artifact is invalid');
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_waveform_series',{
      dataset_id:datasetId,waveform_concept_id:d.waveformConceptId,source_artifact_id:d.sourceArtifactId,
      sample_rate_hz:d.sampleRateHz,channel_count:d.channelCount,duration_ms:d.durationMs,
      sample_encoding:d.sampleEncoding,raw_storage_ref:d.rawStorageRef,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 addWaveformChannel(seriesId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_WAVEFORM_CHANNEL_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:seriesId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const series:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_waveform_series',seriesId,client);
    const count=await client.query<{n:number}>(`SELECT COUNT(*)::int n FROM canonical_waveform_channels WHERE waveform_series_id=$1`,[seriesId]);
    if(Number(count.rows[0]?.n)>=Number(series.channel_count)) throw new ConflictException('Waveform channel count would exceed declared channel_count');
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_waveform_channels',{
      waveform_series_id:seriesId,channel_index:d.channelIndex,canonical_lead_code:d.canonicalLeadCode,
      source_label:d.sourceLabel,physical_unit_ucum:d.physicalUnitUcum,
      gain_to_physical_unit:d.gainToPhysicalUnit,offset_to_physical_unit:d.offsetToPhysicalUnit??0,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 addImagingStudy(datasetId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_IMAGING_STUDY_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:datasetId,
   handler:client=>this.tables.insert('canonical_imaging_studies',{
    dataset_id:datasetId,imaging_concept_id:d.imagingConceptId,study_instance_uid:d.studyInstanceUid,
    modality:d.modality,body_site_concept_id:d.bodySiteConceptId,acquisition_protocol_code:d.acquisitionProtocolCode,
   },client)});
 }

 addImagingSeries(studyId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_IMAGING_SERIES_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:studyId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const study:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_imaging_studies',studyId,client);
    if(study.modality!==d.modality) throw new ConflictException('Series modality must match canonical study modality');
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_imaging_series',{
      canonical_imaging_study_id:studyId,series_instance_uid:d.seriesInstanceUid,modality:d.modality,series_number:d.seriesNumber,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 linkImagingArtifact(seriesId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_IMAGING_ARTIFACT_LINK',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:seriesId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const series:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_imaging_series',seriesId,client);
    const study:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_imaging_studies',series.canonical_imaging_study_id,client);
    const dataset:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_clinical_datasets',study.dataset_id,client);
    const artifact:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_artifacts',d.artifactId,client);
    if(artifact.acquisition_session_id!==dataset.acquisition_session_id || artifact.integrity_status!=='VERIFIED') throw new ConflictException('Imaging artifact is outside dataset acquisition or not verified');
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_imaging_artifact_refs',{
      canonical_imaging_series_id:seriesId,artifact_id:d.artifactId,sop_instance_uid:d.sopInstanceUid,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 addMediaRecording(datasetId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_MEDIA_RECORDING_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:datasetId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const dataset:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_clinical_datasets',datasetId,client);
    const artifact:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_artifacts',d.sourceArtifactId,client);
    if(artifact.acquisition_session_id!==dataset.acquisition_session_id || artifact.integrity_status!=='VERIFIED') throw new ConflictException('Media source artifact is invalid');
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_media_recordings',{
      dataset_id:datasetId,media_concept_id:d.mediaConceptId,source_artifact_id:d.sourceArtifactId,media_type:d.mediaType,
      body_site_concept_id:d.bodySiteConceptId,sample_rate_hz:d.sampleRateHz,channel_count:d.channelCount,codec:d.codec,
      duration_ms:d.durationMs,raw_storage_ref:d.rawStorageRef,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 lifecycle(datasetId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:`CANONICAL_DATASET_${d.eventType}`,resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:datasetId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const dataset:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_clinical_datasets',datasetId,client,true);
    const status=d.eventType==='VOIDED'?'VOID':d.eventType;
    if(d.eventType==='SUPERSEDED' && !d.replacementDatasetId) throw new ConflictException('SUPERSEDED requires replacementDatasetId');
    if(d.replacementDatasetId){
      const replacement:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_clinical_datasets',d.replacementDatasetId,client);
      if(replacement.acquisition_session_id!==dataset.acquisition_session_id) throw new ConflictException('Replacement dataset must belong to same acquisition');
    }
    await this.tables.updateById('canonical_clinical_datasets',datasetId,{dataset_status:status},client);
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_dataset_lifecycle_events',{
      dataset_id:datasetId,event_type:d.eventType,replacement_dataset_id:d.replacementDatasetId,
      reason_code:d.reasonCode,actor_user_id:ctx.userId,actor_component:d.actorComponent,
    },client);
   }});
 }

}

import { ConflictException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { ClinicalRequestContext } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-types';
import { ClinicalCommandExecutor } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-command.executor';
import { ClinicalTableRepository } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-table.repository';

@Injectable()
export class NormalizationService {
 constructor(private readonly tables:ClinicalTableRepository,private readonly commands:ClinicalCommandExecutor){}

 start(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'NORMALIZATION_START',resourceType:'ACQUISITION',resourceId:d.acquisitionSessionId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const profile:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_normalization_profiles',d.normalizationProfileId,client);
    if(profile.profile_status!=='APPROVED') throw new ConflictException('Normalization profile must be APPROVED');

    const artifacts:any[]=d.sourceArtifactIds?.length
      ? await Promise.all(d.sourceArtifactIds.map((id:string)=>this.tables.findById('clinical_artifacts',id,client)))
      : await this.tables.list('clinical_artifacts',{acquisition_session_id:d.acquisitionSessionId},200,client);

    if(!artifacts.length) throw new ConflictException('No source artifacts');
    for(const a of artifacts){
      if(a.acquisition_session_id!==d.acquisitionSessionId) throw new ConflictException('Artifact acquisition mismatch');
      if(a.artifact_status!=='INTEGRITY_VERIFIED' || a.integrity_status!=='VERIFIED'){
        throw new ConflictException(`Artifact ${a.id} is not integrity verified`);
      }
      if(!a.content_sha256) throw new ConflictException(`Artifact ${a.id} has no content SHA-256`);
    }

    const run:any=await this.tables.insert('clinical_normalization_runs',{
      acquisition_session_id:d.acquisitionSessionId,normalization_profile_id:d.normalizationProfileId,
      run_status:'STARTED',normalizer_engine_name:d.normalizerEngineName,normalizer_engine_version:d.normalizerEngineVersion,
      normalizer_build_sha256:d.normalizerBuildSha256,triggered_by_user_id:ctx.userId,triggered_by_component:d.triggeredByComponent,
    },client);

    for(const a of artifacts){
      await this.tables.insert('clinical_normalization_run_sources',{
        normalization_run_id:run.id,artifact_id:a.id,source_artifact_sha256:a.content_sha256,
        source_artifact_status:a.artifact_status,source_storage_ref:a.storage_ref,
      },client);
    }
    return run;
   }});
 }

 complete(runId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'NORMALIZATION_COMPLETE',resourceType:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',resourceId:runId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const run:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_normalization_runs',runId,client,true);
    if(!['STARTED','VALIDATING'].includes(run.run_status)) throw new ConflictException('Normalization run already terminal');
    return this.tables.updateById('clinical_normalization_runs',runId,{
      run_status:d.runStatus,input_fingerprint_sha256:d.inputFingerprintSha256,
      output_fingerprint_sha256:d.outputFingerprintSha256,completed_at:new Date(),
    },client);
   }});
 }

 createDataset(runId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_DATASET_CREATE',resourceType:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',resourceId:runId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const run:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_normalization_runs',runId,client);
    if(!['VALIDATED','REVIEW_REQUIRED','QUARANTINED'].includes(run.run_status)) throw new ConflictException('Run is not ready to publish a dataset');
    const dataset:any=await this.tables.insert('canonical_clinical_datasets',{
      dataset_number:`CDS-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,acquisition_session_id:run.acquisition_session_id,
      normalization_run_id:runId,normalization_profile_id:run.normalization_profile_id,dataset_kind:d.datasetKind,
      canonical_schema_version:d.canonicalSchemaVersion,dataset_status:d.datasetStatus,version_no:d.versionNo,
      supersedes_dataset_id:d.supersedesDatasetId,source_fingerprint_sha256:d.sourceFingerprintSha256,
      canonical_fingerprint_sha256:d.canonicalFingerprintSha256,effective_start_at:d.effectiveStartAt?new Date(d.effectiveStartAt):undefined,
      effective_end_at:d.effectiveEndAt?new Date(d.effectiveEndAt):undefined,normalized_at:new Date(),review_reason_codes:d.reviewReasonCodes??[],
    },client);
    const sources:any[]=await this.tables.list('clinical_normalization_run_sources',{normalization_run_id:runId},200,client);
    for(const s of sources){
      await this.tables.insert('canonical_dataset_source_artifacts',{dataset_id:dataset.id,artifact_id:s.artifact_id,artifact_sha256:s.source_artifact_sha256},client);
    }
    return dataset;
   }});
 }

  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_OBSERVATION_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:datasetId,
   handler:client=>this.tables.insert('canonical_observations',{
    dataset_id:datasetId,canonical_concept_id:d.canonicalConceptId,value_type:d.valueType,value_numeric:d.valueNumeric,
    value_text:d.valueText,value_code:d.valueCode,value_boolean:d.valueBoolean,unit_ucum:d.unitUcum,
    measured_at:d.measuredAt?new Date(d.measuredAt):undefined,source_artifact_id:d.sourceArtifactId,
    source_observation_code:d.sourceObservationCode,source_unit_code:d.sourceUnitCode,

      },client);

  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'NORMALIZATION_COMPLETE',resourceType:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',resourceId:runId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const run:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_normalization_runs',runId,client,true);
    if(!['STARTED','VALIDATING'].includes(run.run_status)) throw new ConflictException('Normalization run already terminal');

   handler:async(client)=>{
    const run:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_normalization_runs',runId,client);
    if(!['VALIDATED','REVIEW_REQUIRED','QUARANTINED'].includes(run.run_status)) throw new ConflictException('Run is not ready to publish a dataset');
    const dataset:any=await this.tables.insert('canonical_clinical_datasets',{
      dataset_number:`CDS-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,acquisition_session_id:run.acquisition_session_id,
      normalization_run_id:runId,normalization_profile_id:run.normalization_profile_id,dataset_kind:d.datasetKind,
      canonical_schema_version:d.canonicalSchemaVersion,dataset_status:d.datasetStatus,version_no:d.versionNo,
      supersedes_dataset_id:d.supersedesDatasetId,source_fingerprint_sha256:d.sourceFingerprintSha256,
      canonical_fingerprint_sha256:d.canonicalFingerprintSha256,effective_start_at:d.effectiveStartAt?new Date(d.effectiveStartAt):undefined,
      effective_end_at:d.effectiveEndAt?new Date(d.effectiveEndAt):undefined,normalized_at:new Date(),review_reason_codes:d.reviewReasonCodes??[],
    },client);
    const sources:any[]=await this.tables.list('clinical_normalization_run_sources',{normalization_run_id:runId},200,client);
    for(const s of sources){
      await this.tables.insert('canonical_dataset_source_artifacts',{dataset_id:dataset.id,artifact_id:s.artifact_id,artifact_sha256:s.source_artifact_sha256},client);
    }
    return dataset;
   }});
 }

 addObservation(datasetId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_OBSERVATION_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:datasetId,
   handler:client=>this.tables.insert('canonical_observations',{
    dataset_id:datasetId,canonical_concept_id:d.canonicalConceptId,value_type:d.valueType,value_numeric:d.valueNumeric,
    value_text:d.valueText,value_code:d.valueCode,value_boolean:d.valueBoolean,unit_ucum:d.unitUcum,
    measured_at:d.measuredAt?new Date(d.measuredAt):undefined,source_artifact_id:d.sourceArtifactId,
    source_observation_code:d.sourceObservationCode,source_unit_code:d.sourceUnitCode,
    normalization_rule_id:d.normalizationRuleId,mapping_rule_sha256:d.mappingRuleSha256,
   },client)});
 }
 addWaveformSeries(datasetId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_WAVEFORM_SERIES_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:datasetId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const dataset:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_clinical_datasets',datasetId,client);
    if(!['VALIDATED','REVIEW_REQUIRED'].includes(dataset.dataset_status)) throw new ConflictException('Dataset is not publishable');
    const artifact:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_artifacts',d.sourceArtifactId,client);
    if(artifact.acquisition_session_id!==dataset.acquisition_session_id || artifact.integrity_status!=='VERIFIED') throw new ConflictException('Waveform source artifact is invalid');
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_waveform_series',{
      dataset_id:datasetId,waveform_concept_id:d.waveformConceptId,source_artifact_id:d.sourceArtifactId,
      sample_rate_hz:d.sampleRateHz,channel_count:d.channelCount,duration_ms:d.durationMs,
      sample_encoding:d.sampleEncoding,raw_storage_ref:d.rawStorageRef,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 addWaveformChannel(seriesId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_WAVEFORM_CHANNEL_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:seriesId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const series:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_waveform_series',seriesId,client);
    const count=await client.query<{n:number}>(`SELECT COUNT(*)::int n FROM canonical_waveform_channels WHERE waveform_series_id=$1`,[seriesId]);
    if(Number(count.rows[0]?.n)>=Number(series.channel_count)) throw new ConflictException('Waveform channel count would exceed declared channel_count');
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_waveform_channels',{
      waveform_series_id:seriesId,channel_index:d.channelIndex,canonical_lead_code:d.canonicalLeadCode,
      source_label:d.sourceLabel,physical_unit_ucum:d.physicalUnitUcum,
      gain_to_physical_unit:d.gainToPhysicalUnit,offset_to_physical_unit:d.offsetToPhysicalUnit??0,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 addImagingStudy(datasetId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_IMAGING_STUDY_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:datasetId,
   handler:client=>this.tables.insert('canonical_imaging_studies',{
    dataset_id:datasetId,imaging_concept_id:d.imagingConceptId,study_instance_uid:d.studyInstanceUid,
    modality:d.modality,body_site_concept_id:d.bodySiteConceptId,acquisition_protocol_code:d.acquisitionProtocolCode,
   },client)});
 }

 addImagingSeries(studyId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_IMAGING_SERIES_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:studyId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const study:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_imaging_studies',studyId,client);
    if(study.modality!==d.modality) throw new ConflictException('Series modality must match canonical study modality');
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_imaging_series',{
      canonical_imaging_study_id:studyId,series_instance_uid:d.seriesInstanceUid,modality:d.modality,series_number:d.seriesNumber,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 linkImagingArtifact(seriesId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_IMAGING_ARTIFACT_LINK',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:seriesId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const series:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_imaging_series',seriesId,client);
    const study:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_imaging_studies',series.canonical_imaging_study_id,client);
    const dataset:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_clinical_datasets',study.dataset_id,client);
    const artifact:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_artifacts',d.artifactId,client);
    if(artifact.acquisition_session_id!==dataset.acquisition_session_id || artifact.integrity_status!=='VERIFIED') throw new ConflictException('Imaging artifact is outside dataset acquisition or not verified');
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_imaging_artifact_refs',{
      canonical_imaging_series_id:seriesId,artifact_id:d.artifactId,sop_instance_uid:d.sopInstanceUid,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 addMediaRecording(datasetId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:'CANONICAL_MEDIA_RECORDING_ADD',resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:datasetId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const dataset:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_clinical_datasets',datasetId,client);
    const artifact:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_artifacts',d.sourceArtifactId,client);
    if(artifact.acquisition_session_id!==dataset.acquisition_session_id || artifact.integrity_status!=='VERIFIED') throw new ConflictException('Media source artifact is invalid');
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_media_recordings',{
      dataset_id:datasetId,media_concept_id:d.mediaConceptId,source_artifact_id:d.sourceArtifactId,media_type:d.mediaType,
      body_site_concept_id:d.bodySiteConceptId,sample_rate_hz:d.sampleRateHz,channel_count:d.channelCount,codec:d.codec,
      duration_ms:d.durationMs,raw_storage_ref:d.rawStorageRef,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 lifecycle(datasetId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'NORMALIZATION_RUN',action:`CANONICAL_DATASET_${d.eventType}`,resourceType:'CANONICAL_DATASET',resourceId:datasetId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const dataset:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_clinical_datasets',datasetId,client,true);
    const status=d.eventType==='VOIDED'?'VOID':d.eventType;
    if(d.eventType==='SUPERSEDED' && !d.replacementDatasetId) throw new ConflictException('SUPERSEDED requires replacementDatasetId');
    if(d.replacementDatasetId){
      const replacement:any=await this.tables.findById('canonical_clinical_datasets',d.replacementDatasetId,client);
      if(replacement.acquisition_session_id!==dataset.acquisition_session_id) throw new ConflictException('Replacement dataset must belong to same acquisition');
    }
    await this.tables.updateById('canonical_clinical_datasets',datasetId,{dataset_status:status},client);
    return this.tables.insert('canonical_dataset_lifecycle_events',{
      dataset_id:datasetId,event_type:d.eventType,replacement_dataset_id:d.replacementDatasetId,
      reason_code:d.reasonCode,actor_user_id:ctx.userId,actor_component:d.actorComponent,
    },client);
   }});
 }

}

import { Body,Controller,Param,Post,Req,UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClinicalAuthenticationGuard } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-authentication.guard';import { ClinicalRequestContextService } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-request-context.service';
import { InterpretationService } from './interpretation.service';
import { AddFindingDto,AmendReportDto,AssignInterpretationDto,CreateInterpretationDto,CreateReportDto,CriticalResultDto,SignReportDto,ReportSectionDto,ReportArtifactLinkDto,CriticalDeliveryDto,InterpretationAuditDto } from './interpretation.dto';
@UseGuards(ClinicalAuthenticationGuard) @Controller('clinical')
export class InterpretationController{
 constructor(private readonly s:InterpretationService,private readonly c:ClinicalRequestContextService){}
 @Post('interpretations') create(@Body() d:CreateInterpretationDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.create(d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('interpretations/:id/assign') assign(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AssignInterpretationDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.assign(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('interpretations/:id/start') start(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:any,@Req() r:any){return this.s.start(id,d.expectedVersion,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('interpretations/:id/findings') finding(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AddFindingDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addFinding(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('diagnostic-reports') report(@Body() d:CreateReportDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.createReport(d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('diagnostic-reports/:id/sign') sign(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:SignReportDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.signReport(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('diagnostic-reports/amend') amend(@Body() d:AmendReportDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.amendReport(d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('critical-results') critical(@Body() d:CriticalResultDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.createCritical(d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('critical-results/:id/acknowledge') ack(@Param('id') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.acknowledgeCritical(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('diagnostic-reports/:id/sections') section(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:ReportSectionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addReportSection(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('diagnostic-reports/:id/artifacts') link(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:ReportArtifactLinkDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.linkReportArtifact(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('critical-results/:id/delivered') delivered(@Param('id') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.markCriticalDelivered(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('critical-results/:id/escalate') critEsc(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CriticalDeliveryDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.escalateCritical(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('critical-results/:id/close') critClose(@Param('id') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.closeCritical(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('interpretations/:id/audit-events') ia(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:InterpretationAuditDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.interpretationAudit(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}

}

import { IsIn,IsInt,IsOptional,IsString,IsUUID,Min } from '../../clinical-common/dto';
export class CreateInterpretationDto{
 @IsUUID() careEncounterId!:string; @IsUUID() examOrderId!:string; @IsUUID() acceptedAcquisitionId!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() canonicalDatasetId?:string;
}
export class AssignInterpretationDto{ @IsUUID() interpreterWorkforceId!:string; @IsUUID() interpreterCapabilityGrantId!:string; @IsInt() @Min(1) expectedVersion!:number; }
export class AddFindingDto{
 @IsString() findingCode!:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() findingCodingSystem?:string; @IsString() findingDisplay!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsIn(['CONFIRMED','PROBABLE','POSSIBLE','UNDETERMINED','NOT_APPLICABLE']) certainty?:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsIn(['NORMAL','MILD','MODERATE','SEVERE','CRITICAL','UNKNOWN']) severity?:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() bodySiteCode?:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() sourceArtifactId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() sourceDatasetId?:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() narrativeRef?:string;
}
export class CreateReportDto{ @IsUUID() interpretationCaseId!:string; @IsString() title!:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() impressionRef?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() narrativeRef?:string; }
export class SignReportDto{ @IsUUID() signingCapabilityGrantId!:string; @IsString() reportFingerprintSha256!:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() reasonCode?:string; }
export class AmendReportDto extends CreateReportDto { @IsUUID() supersedesReportId!:string; @IsUUID() signingCapabilityGrantId!:string; @IsString() reportFingerprintSha256!:string; @IsIn(['AMENDED','CORRECTED']) reportStatus!:string; @IsString() reasonCode!:string; }
export class CriticalResultDto{
 @IsUUID() interpretationCaseId!:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() diagnosticReportId?:string; @IsIn(['HIGH','CRITICAL']) severity!:string;
 @IsString() triggerCode!:string; @IsIn(['CLINICIAN_INTERPRETATION','VALIDATED_RULE_SUPPORT','EXTERNAL_LAB','OTHER']) triggerSource!:string; @IsString() messageRef!:string;
}

export class ReportSectionDto {
 @IsString() sectionCode!:string; @IsString() displayName!:string; @IsInt() @Min(1) sequenceNo!:number;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() contentRef?:string; @IsOptional() structuredContent?:Record<string,unknown>;
}
export class ReportArtifactLinkDto {
 @IsUUID() clinicalArtifactId!:string;
 @IsIn(['PRIMARY_SOURCE','SUPPORTING_SOURCE','KEY_IMAGE','KEY_WAVEFORM','ATTACHMENT']) linkRole!:string;
}
export class CriticalDeliveryDto {
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() acknowledgedByWorkforceId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() escalationRef?:string;
}
export class InterpretationAuditDto {
 @IsString() action!:string; @IsString() targetEntityType!:string; @IsString() targetEntityId!:string; @IsString() reasonCode!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() previousFingerprint?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() newFingerprint?:string;
}

 @IsOptional() @IsString() contentRef?:string; @IsOptional() structuredContent?:Record<string,unknown>;
}
export class ReportArtifactLinkDto {
 @IsUUID() clinicalArtifactId!:string;
 @IsIn(['PRIMARY_SOURCE','SUPPORTING_SOURCE','KEY_IMAGE','KEY_WAVEFORM','ATTACHMENT']) linkRole!:string;
}
export class CriticalDeliveryDto {
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() acknowledgedByWorkforceId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() escalationRef?:string;
}
export class InterpretationAuditDto {
 @IsString() action!:string; @IsString() targetEntityType!:string; @IsString() targetEntityId!:string; @IsString() reasonCode!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() previousFingerprint?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() newFingerprint?:string;
}

import { IsIn,IsInt,IsOptional,IsString,IsUUID,Min } from '../../clinical-common/dto';
export class CreateInterpretationDto{
 @IsUUID() careEncounterId!:string; @IsUUID() examOrderId!:string; @IsUUID() acceptedAcquisitionId!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() canonicalDatasetId?:string;
}
export class AssignInterpretationDto{ @IsUUID() interpreterWorkforceId!:string; @IsUUID() interpreterCapabilityGrantId!:string; @IsInt() @Min(1) expectedVersion!:number; }
export class AddFindingDto{
 @IsString() findingCode!:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() findingCodingSystem?:string; @IsString() findingDisplay!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsIn(['CONFIRMED','PROBABLE','POSSIBLE','UNDETERMINED','NOT_APPLICABLE']) certainty?:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsIn(['NORMAL','MILD','MODERATE','SEVERE','CRITICAL','UNKNOWN']) severity?:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() bodySiteCode?:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() sourceArtifactId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() sourceDatasetId?:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() narrativeRef?:string;
}
export class CreateReportDto{ @IsUUID() interpretationCaseId!:string; @IsString() title!:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() impressionRef?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() narrativeRef?:string; }
export class SignReportDto{ @IsUUID() signingCapabilityGrantId!:string; @IsString() reportFingerprintSha256!:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() reasonCode?:string; }
export class AmendReportDto extends CreateReportDto { @IsUUID() supersedesReportId!:string; @IsUUID() signingCapabilityGrantId!:string; @IsString() reportFingerprintSha256!:string; @IsIn(['AMENDED','CORRECTED']) reportStatus!:string; @IsString() reasonCode!:string; }
export class CriticalResultDto{
 @IsUUID() interpretationCaseId!:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() diagnosticReportId?:string; @IsIn(['HIGH','CRITICAL']) severity!:string;
 @IsString() triggerCode!:string; @IsIn(['CLINICIAN_INTERPRETATION','VALIDATED_RULE_SUPPORT','EXTERNAL_LAB','OTHER']) triggerSource!:string; @IsString() messageRef!:string;
}

export class ReportSectionDto {
 @IsString() sectionCode!:string; @IsString() displayName!:string; @IsInt() @Min(1) sequenceNo!:number;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() contentRef?:string; @IsOptional() structuredContent?:Record<string,unknown>;
}
export class ReportArtifactLinkDto {
 @IsUUID() clinicalArtifactId!:string;
 @IsIn(['PRIMARY_SOURCE','SUPPORTING_SOURCE','KEY_IMAGE','KEY_WAVEFORM','ATTACHMENT']) linkRole!:string;
}
export class CriticalDeliveryDto {
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() acknowledgedByWorkforceId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() escalationRef?:string;
}
export class InterpretationAuditDto {
 @IsString() action!:string; @IsString() targetEntityType!:string; @IsString() targetEntityId!:string; @IsString() reasonCode!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() previousFingerprint?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() newFingerprint?:string;
}

import { ConflictException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { ClinicalRequestContext } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-types';
import { ClinicalCommandExecutor } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-command.executor';
import { ClinicalTableRepository } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-table.repository';
import { WorkforceCapabilityService } from '../../clinical-common/workforce-capability.service';

@Injectable()
export class InterpretationService{
 constructor(private readonly tables:ClinicalTableRepository,private readonly commands:ClinicalCommandExecutor,private readonly capabilities:WorkforceCapabilityService){}

 create(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'INTERPRETATION_CREATE',resourceType:'INTERPRETATION',
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const acq:any=await this.tables.findById('exam_acquisition_sessions',d.acceptedAcquisitionId,client);
    if(acq.acquisition_status!=='QUALITY_ACCEPTED') throw new ConflictException('Interpretation requires QUALITY_ACCEPTED acquisition');
    return this.tables.insert('clinical_interpretation_cases',{
      case_number:`INT-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,care_encounter_id:d.careEncounterId,exam_order_id:d.examOrderId,
      accepted_acquisition_id:d.acceptedAcquisitionId,canonical_dataset_id:d.canonicalDatasetId,created_by_user_id:ctx.userId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 assign(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'INTERPRETATION_ASSIGN',resourceType:'INTERPRETATION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',id,client,true);
    const order:any=await this.tables.findById('exam_orders',row.exam_order_id,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.interpreterCapabilityGrantId,d.interpreterWorkforceId,'INTERPRET_CLINICAL_EXAM_RESULT',order.target_site_id);
    if(!['PENDING','ASSIGNED'].includes(row.interpretation_status)) throw new ConflictException('Interpretation cannot be reassigned now');
    return this.tables.updateById('clinical_interpretation_cases',id,{
      interpretation_status:'ASSIGNED',assigned_interpreter_workforce_id:d.interpreterWorkforceId,
      interpreter_capability_grant_id:d.interpreterCapabilityGrantId,assigned_at:new Date(),version:Number(row.version)+1,
    },client,d.expectedVersion);
   }});
 }

 start(id:string,expectedVersion:number,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'INTERPRETATION_START',resourceType:'INTERPRETATION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',id,client,true);
    if(row.assigned_interpreter_workforce_id!==ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Only assigned interpreter can start');
    return this.tables.updateById('clinical_interpretation_cases',id,{interpretation_status:'IN_REVIEW',started_at:new Date(),version:Number(row.version)+1},client,expectedVersion);
   }});
 }

 addFinding(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'INTERPRETATION_FINDING_ADD',resourceType:'INTERPRETATION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const c:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',id,client);
    if(c.assigned_interpreter_workforce_id && c.assigned_interpreter_workforce_id!==ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Not assigned interpreter');
    return this.tables.insert('clinical_findings',{
      interpretation_case_id:id,finding_code:d.findingCode,finding_coding_system:d.findingCodingSystem,finding_display:d.findingDisplay,
      certainty:d.certainty,severity:d.severity,body_site_code:d.bodySiteCode,source_artifact_id:d.sourceArtifactId,source_dataset_id:d.sourceDatasetId,
      narrative_ref:d.narrativeRef,created_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 createReport(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'REPORT_CREATE',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const existing:any[]=await this.tables.list('diagnostic_reports',{interpretation_case_id:d.interpretationCaseId},100,client);
    const version=Math.max(0,...existing.map(x=>Number(x.report_version)))+1;
    return this.tables.insert('diagnostic_reports',{
      report_number:`RPT-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,interpretation_case_id:d.interpretationCaseId,report_version:version,
      report_status:'DRAFT',title:d.title,impression_ref:d.impressionRef,narrative_ref:d.narrativeRef,created_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 signReport(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_REPORT_SIGN',action:'REPORT_SIGN',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const report:any=await this.tables.findById('diagnostic_reports',id,client,true);
    const icase:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',report.interpretation_case_id,client);
    const order:any=await this.tables.findById('exam_orders',icase.exam_order_id,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.signingCapabilityGrantId,ctx.workforceId,'SIGN_DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',order.target_site_id);
    if(!['DRAFT','PRELIMINARY'].includes(report.report_status)) throw new ConflictException('Report is not signable');
    const updated=await this.tables.updateById('diagnostic_reports',id,{
      report_status:'FINAL',signed_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,signing_capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,signed_at:new Date(),
    },client);
    await this.tables.insert('report_signing_events',{
      diagnostic_report_id:id,event_type:'SIGNED_FINAL',actor_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,
      report_fingerprint_sha256:d.reportFingerprintSha256,reason_code:d.reasonCode??'FINAL_REPORT_SIGNED',
    },client);
    const c:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',report.interpretation_case_id,client,true);
    await this.tables.updateById('clinical_interpretation_cases',c.id,{interpretation_status:'SIGNED',signed_at:new Date(),version:Number(c.version)+1},client);
    return updated;
   }});
 }

 amendReport(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_REPORT_SIGN',action:'REPORT_AMEND',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',resourceId:d.supersedesReportId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const prev:any=await this.tables.findById('diagnostic_reports',d.supersedesReportId,client);
    const icase:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',prev.interpretation_case_id,client);
    const order:any=await this.tables.findById('exam_orders',icase.exam_order_id,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.signingCapabilityGrantId,ctx.workforceId,'SIGN_DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',order.target_site_id);
    if(!['FINAL','AMENDED','CORRECTED'].includes(prev.report_status)) throw new ConflictException('Only signed report may be superseded');
    const next:any=await this.tables.insert('diagnostic_reports',{
      report_number:`RPT-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,interpretation_case_id:prev.interpretation_case_id,
      report_version:Number(prev.report_version)+1,report_status:d.reportStatus,title:d.title,impression_ref:d.impressionRef,narrative_ref:d.narrativeRef,
      signed_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,signing_capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,signed_at:new Date(),
      supersedes_report_id:prev.id,created_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
    },client);
    await this.tables.insert('report_signing_events',{
      diagnostic_report_id:next.id,event_type:d.reportStatus==='CORRECTED'?'CORRECTED':'AMENDED',actor_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
      capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,report_fingerprint_sha256:d.reportFingerprintSha256,reason_code:d.reasonCode,
    },client);
    return next;
   }});
 }

 createCritical(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_CREATE',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    return this.tables.insert('critical_result_notifications',{
      interpretation_case_id:d.interpretationCaseId,diagnostic_report_id:d.diagnosticReportId,severity:d.severity,
      trigger_code:d.triggerCode,trigger_source:d.triggerSource,message_ref:d.messageRef,created_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 acknowledgeCritical(id:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_ACK',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('critical_result_notifications',id,client,true);
    if(!['OPEN','DELIVERED','ESCALATED'].includes(row.notification_status)) throw new ConflictException('Critical result is not open');
    return this.tables.updateById('critical_result_notifications',id,{
      notification_status:'ACKNOWLEDGED',acknowledged_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,acknowledged_at:new Date(),
    },client);
   }});
 }
 addReportSection(reportId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'REPORT_SECTION_ADD',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',resourceId:reportId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const report:any=await this.tables.findById('diagnostic_reports',reportId,client);
    if(report.report_status!=='DRAFT') throw new ConflictException('Report sections are editable only while DRAFT');
    return this.tables.insert('diagnostic_report_sections',{
      diagnostic_report_id:reportId,section_code:d.sectionCode,display_name:d.displayName,
      content_ref:d.contentRef,structured_content:d.structuredContent,sequence_no:d.sequenceNo,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 linkReportArtifact(reportId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'REPORT_ARTIFACT_LINK',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',resourceId:reportId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const report:any=await this.tables.findById('diagnostic_reports',reportId,client);
    if(report.report_status!=='DRAFT') throw new ConflictException('Artifact links are editable only while DRAFT');
    const icase:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',report.interpretation_case_id,client);
    const artifact:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_artifacts',d.clinicalArtifactId,client);
    if(artifact.acquisition_session_id!==icase.accepted_acquisition_id) throw new ConflictException('Report artifact must belong to accepted acquisition');
    return this.tables.insert('diagnostic_report_artifact_links',{
      diagnostic_report_id:reportId,clinical_artifact_id:d.clinicalArtifactId,link_role:d.linkRole,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 markCriticalDelivered(id:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_DELIVERED',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('critical_result_notifications',id,client,true);
    if(row.notification_status!=='OPEN') throw new ConflictException('Only OPEN critical result can be marked delivered');
    return this.tables.updateById('critical_result_notifications',id,{notification_status:'DELIVERED'},client);
   }});
 }

 escalateCritical(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_ESCALATE',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('critical_result_notifications',id,client,true);
    if(!['OPEN','DELIVERED'].includes(row.notification_status)) throw new ConflictException('Critical result is not escalatable');
    if(!d.escalationRef) throw new ConflictException('escalationRef is required');
    return this.tables.updateById('critical_result_notifications',id,{notification_status:'ESCALATED',escalation_ref:d.escalationRef},client);
   }});
 }

 closeCritical(id:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_CLOSE',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('critical_result_notifications',id,client,true);
    if(!['ACKNOWLEDGED','ESCALATED'].includes(row.notification_status)) throw new ConflictException('Critical result must be acknowledged or escalated before close');
    return this.tables.updateById('critical_result_notifications',id,{notification_status:'CLOSED'},client);
   }});
 }

 interpretationAudit(caseId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'INTERPRETATION_AUDIT_RECORD',resourceType:'INTERPRETATION',resourceId:caseId,
   handler:client=>this.tables.insert('clinical_interpretation_audit_events',{
     interpretation_case_id:caseId,action:d.action,actor_user_id:ctx.userId,actor_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
     target_entity_type:d.targetEntityType,target_entity_id:d.targetEntityId,reason_code:d.reasonCode,
     previous_fingerprint:d.previousFingerprint,new_fingerprint:d.newFingerprint,
   },client)});
 }

}

    },client);

 }

 signReport(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_REPORT_SIGN',action:'REPORT_SIGN',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const report:any=await this.tables.findById('diagnostic_reports',id,client,true);
    const icase:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',report.interpretation_case_id,client);
    const order:any=await this.tables.findById('exam_orders',icase.exam_order_id,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.signingCapabilityGrantId,ctx.workforceId,'SIGN_DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',order.target_site_id);
    if(!['DRAFT','PRELIMINARY'].includes(report.report_status)) throw new ConflictException('Report is not signable');
    const updated=await this.tables.updateById('diagnostic_reports',id,{
      report_status:'FINAL',signed_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,signing_capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,signed_at:new Date(),
    },client);
    await this.tables.insert('report_signing_events',{
      diagnostic_report_id:id,event_type:'SIGNED_FINAL',actor_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,
      report_fingerprint_sha256:d.reportFingerprintSha256,reason_code:d.reasonCode??'FINAL_REPORT_SIGNED',
    },client);
    const c:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',report.interpretation_case_id,client,true);
    await this.tables.updateById('clinical_interpretation_cases',c.id,{interpretation_status:'SIGNED',signed_at:new Date(),version:Number(c.version)+1},client);
    return updated;
   }});
 }

 amendReport(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_REPORT_SIGN',action:'REPORT_AMEND',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',resourceId:d.supersedesReportId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const prev:any=await this.tables.findById('diagnostic_reports',d.supersedesReportId,client);
    const icase:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',prev.interpretation_case_id,client);
    const order:any=await this.tables.findById('exam_orders',icase.exam_order_id,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.signingCapabilityGrantId,ctx.workforceId,'SIGN_DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',order.target_site_id);
    if(!['FINAL','AMENDED','CORRECTED'].includes(prev.report_status)) throw new ConflictException('Only signed report may be superseded');
    const next:any=await this.tables.insert('diagnostic_reports',{
      report_number:`RPT-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,interpretation_case_id:prev.interpretation_case_id,
      report_version:Number(prev.report_version)+1,report_status:d.reportStatus,title:d.title,impression_ref:d.impressionRef,narrative_ref:d.narrativeRef,
      signed_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,signing_capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,signed_at:new Date(),
      supersedes_report_id:prev.id,created_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
    },client);
    await this.tables.insert('report_signing_events',{
      diagnostic_report_id:next.id,event_type:d.reportStatus==='CORRECTED'?'CORRECTED':'AMENDED',actor_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
      capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,report_fingerprint_sha256:d.reportFingerprintSha256,reason_code:d.reasonCode,
    },client);
    return next;
   }});
 }

 createCritical(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_CREATE',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    return this.tables.insert('critical_result_notifications',{
      interpretation_case_id:d.interpretationCaseId,diagnostic_report_id:d.diagnosticReportId,severity:d.severity,
      trigger_code:d.triggerCode,trigger_source:d.triggerSource,message_ref:d.messageRef,created_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 acknowledgeCritical(id:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_ACK',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('critical_result_notifications',id,client,true);
    if(!['OPEN','DELIVERED','ESCALATED'].includes(row.notification_status)) throw new ConflictException('Critical result is not open');
    return this.tables.updateById('critical_result_notifications',id,{
      notification_status:'ACKNOWLEDGED',acknowledged_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,acknowledged_at:new Date(),
    },client);
   }});
 }
 addReportSection(reportId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'REPORT_SECTION_ADD',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',resourceId:reportId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const report:any=await this.tables.findById('diagnostic_reports',reportId,client);
    if(report.report_status!=='DRAFT') throw new ConflictException('Report sections are editable only while DRAFT');
    return this.tables.insert('diagnostic_report_sections',{
      diagnostic_report_id:reportId,section_code:d.sectionCode,display_name:d.displayName,
      content_ref:d.contentRef,structured_content:d.structuredContent,sequence_no:d.sequenceNo,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 linkReportArtifact(reportId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'REPORT_ARTIFACT_LINK',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',resourceId:reportId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const report:any=await this.tables.findById('diagnostic_reports',reportId,client);
    if(report.report_status!=='DRAFT') throw new ConflictException('Artifact links are editable only while DRAFT');
    const icase:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',report.interpretation_case_id,client);
    const artifact:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_artifacts',d.clinicalArtifactId,client);
    if(artifact.acquisition_session_id!==icase.accepted_acquisition_id) throw new ConflictException('Report artifact must belong to accepted acquisition');
    return this.tables.insert('diagnostic_report_artifact_links',{
      diagnostic_report_id:reportId,clinical_artifact_id:d.clinicalArtifactId,link_role:d.linkRole,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 markCriticalDelivered(id:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_DELIVERED',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('critical_result_notifications',id,client,true);
    if(row.notification_status!=='OPEN') throw new ConflictException('Only OPEN critical result can be marked delivered');
    return this.tables.updateById('critical_result_notifications',id,{notification_status:'DELIVERED'},client);
   }});
 }

 escalateCritical(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_ESCALATE',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('critical_result_notifications',id,client,true);
    if(!['OPEN','DELIVERED'].includes(row.notification_status)) throw new ConflictException('Critical result is not escalatable');
    if(!d.escalationRef) throw new ConflictException('escalationRef is required');
    return this.tables.updateById('critical_result_notifications',id,{notification_status:'ESCALATED',escalation_ref:d.escalationRef},client);
   }});
 }

 closeCritical(id:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_CLOSE',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('critical_result_notifications',id,client,true);
    if(!['ACKNOWLEDGED','ESCALATED'].includes(row.notification_status)) throw new ConflictException('Critical result must be acknowledged or escalated before close');
    return this.tables.updateById('critical_result_notifications',id,{notification_status:'CLOSED'},client);
   }});
 }

   },client)});

import { ConflictException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { ClinicalRequestContext } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-types';
import { ClinicalCommandExecutor } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-command.executor';
import { ClinicalTableRepository } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-table.repository';
import { WorkforceCapabilityService } from '../../clinical-common/workforce-capability.service';

@Injectable()
export class InterpretationService{
 constructor(private readonly tables:ClinicalTableRepository,private readonly commands:ClinicalCommandExecutor,private readonly capabilities:WorkforceCapabilityService){}

 create(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'INTERPRETATION_CREATE',resourceType:'INTERPRETATION',
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const acq:any=await this.tables.findById('exam_acquisition_sessions',d.acceptedAcquisitionId,client);
    if(acq.acquisition_status!=='QUALITY_ACCEPTED') throw new ConflictException('Interpretation requires QUALITY_ACCEPTED acquisition');
    return this.tables.insert('clinical_interpretation_cases',{
      case_number:`INT-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,care_encounter_id:d.careEncounterId,exam_order_id:d.examOrderId,
      accepted_acquisition_id:d.acceptedAcquisitionId,canonical_dataset_id:d.canonicalDatasetId,created_by_user_id:ctx.userId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 assign(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'INTERPRETATION_ASSIGN',resourceType:'INTERPRETATION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',id,client,true);
    const order:any=await this.tables.findById('exam_orders',row.exam_order_id,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.interpreterCapabilityGrantId,d.interpreterWorkforceId,'INTERPRET_CLINICAL_EXAM_RESULT',order.target_site_id);
    if(!['PENDING','ASSIGNED'].includes(row.interpretation_status)) throw new ConflictException('Interpretation cannot be reassigned now');
    return this.tables.updateById('clinical_interpretation_cases',id,{
      interpretation_status:'ASSIGNED',assigned_interpreter_workforce_id:d.interpreterWorkforceId,
      interpreter_capability_grant_id:d.interpreterCapabilityGrantId,assigned_at:new Date(),version:Number(row.version)+1,
    },client,d.expectedVersion);
   }});
 }

 start(id:string,expectedVersion:number,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'INTERPRETATION_START',resourceType:'INTERPRETATION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const row:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',id,client,true);
    if(row.assigned_interpreter_workforce_id!==ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Only assigned interpreter can start');
    return this.tables.updateById('clinical_interpretation_cases',id,{interpretation_status:'IN_REVIEW',started_at:new Date(),version:Number(row.version)+1},client,expectedVersion);
   }});
 }

 addFinding(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'INTERPRETATION_FINDING_ADD',resourceType:'INTERPRETATION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const c:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',id,client);
    if(c.assigned_interpreter_workforce_id && c.assigned_interpreter_workforce_id!==ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Not assigned interpreter');
    return this.tables.insert('clinical_findings',{
      interpretation_case_id:id,finding_code:d.findingCode,finding_coding_system:d.findingCodingSystem,finding_display:d.findingDisplay,
      certainty:d.certainty,severity:d.severity,body_site_code:d.bodySiteCode,source_artifact_id:d.sourceArtifactId,source_dataset_id:d.sourceDatasetId,
      narrative_ref:d.narrativeRef,created_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 createReport(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'REPORT_CREATE',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const existing:any[]=await this.tables.list('diagnostic_reports',{interpretation_case_id:d.interpretationCaseId},100,client);
    const version=Math.max(0,...existing.map(x=>Number(x.report_version)))+1;
    return this.tables.insert('diagnostic_reports',{
      report_number:`RPT-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,interpretation_case_id:d.interpretationCaseId,report_version:version,
      report_status:'DRAFT',title:d.title,impression_ref:d.impressionRef,narrative_ref:d.narrativeRef,created_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 signReport(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_REPORT_SIGN',action:'REPORT_SIGN',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const report:any=await this.tables.findById('diagnostic_reports',id,client,true);
    const icase:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',report.interpretation_case_id,client);
    const order:any=await this.tables.findById('exam_orders',icase.exam_order_id,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.signingCapabilityGrantId,ctx.workforceId,'SIGN_DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',order.target_site_id);
    if(!['DRAFT','PRELIMINARY'].includes(report.report_status)) throw new ConflictException('Report is not signable');
    const updated=await this.tables.updateById('diagnostic_reports',id,{
      report_status:'FINAL',signed_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,signing_capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,signed_at:new Date(),
    },client);
    await this.tables.insert('report_signing_events',{
      diagnostic_report_id:id,event_type:'SIGNED_FINAL',actor_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,
      report_fingerprint_sha256:d.reportFingerprintSha256,reason_code:d.reasonCode??'FINAL_REPORT_SIGNED',
    },client);
    const c:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',report.interpretation_case_id,client,true);
    await this.tables.updateById('clinical_interpretation_cases',c.id,{interpretation_status:'SIGNED',signed_at:new Date(),version:Number(c.version)+1},client);
    return updated;
   }});
 }

 amendReport(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_REPORT_SIGN',action:'REPORT_AMEND',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',resourceId:d.supersedesReportId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const prev:any=await this.tables.findById('diagnostic_reports',d.supersedesReportId,client);
    const icase:any=await this.tables.findById('clinical_interpretation_cases',prev.interpretation_case_id,client);
    const order:any=await this.tables.findById('exam_orders',icase.exam_order_id,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.signingCapabilityGrantId,ctx.workforceId,'SIGN_DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',order.target_site_id);
    if(!['FINAL','AMENDED','CORRECTED'].includes(prev.report_status)) throw new ConflictException('Only signed report may be superseded');
    const next:any=await this.tables.insert('diagnostic_reports',{
      report_number:`RPT-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,interpretation_case_id:prev.interpretation_case_id,
      report_version:Number(prev.report_version)+1,report_status:d.reportStatus,title:d.title,impression_ref:d.impressionRef,narrative_ref:d.narrativeRef,
      signed_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,signing_capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,signed_at:new Date(),
      supersedes_report_id:prev.id,created_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
    },client);
    await this.tables.insert('report_signing_events',{
      diagnostic_report_id:next.id,event_type:d.reportStatus==='CORRECTED'?'CORRECTED':'AMENDED',actor_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
      capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,report_fingerprint_sha256:d.reportFingerprintSha256,reason_code:d.reasonCode,
    },client);
    return next;
   }});
 }

 createCritical(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_CREATE',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    return this.tables.insert('critical_result_notifications',{
      interpretation_case_id:d.interpretationCaseId,diagnostic_report_id:d.diagnosticReportId,severity:d.severity,
      trigger_code:d.triggerCode,trigger_source:d.triggerSource,message_ref:d.messageRef,created_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 acknowledgeCritical(id:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'CRITICAL_RESULT_ACK',resourceType:'CRITICAL_RESULT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
