    if(!['OPEN','DELIVERED','ESCALATED'].includes(row.notification_status)) throw new ConflictException('Critical result is not open');
    return this.tables.updateById('critical_result_notifications',id,{
      notification_status:'ACKNOWLEDGED',acknowledged_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,acknowledged_at:new Date(),
    },client);
   }});
 }
 addReportSection(reportId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_INTERPRET',action:'REPORT_SECTION_ADD',resourceType:'DIAGNOSTIC_REPORT',resourceId:reportId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const report:any=await this.tables.findById('diagnostic_reports',reportId,client);

    return this.tables.insert('diagnostic_report_sections',{
      diagnostic_report_id:reportId,section_code:d.sectionCode,display_name:d.displayName,
      content_ref:d.contentRef,structured_content:d.structuredContent,sequence_no:d.sequenceNo,
    },client);
   }});
 }

 linkReportArtifact(reportId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){

   handler:async(client)=>{

      diagnostic_report_id:reportId,clinical_artifact_id:d.clinicalArtifactId,link_role:d.linkRole,
    },client);

 }

import { Body,Controller,Get,Param,Post,Req,UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClinicalAuthenticationGuard } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-authentication.guard';import { ClinicalRequestContextService } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-request-context.service';
import { PrescriptionService } from './prescription.service';import { AddMedicationDto,CreatePrescriptionDto,ResolveSafetyAlertDto,SafetyCheckDto,SignPrescriptionDto,TransmitPrescriptionDto,CancelPrescriptionDto,PharmacyFulfillmentDto,MedicationReceiptDto } from './prescription.dto';
@UseGuards(ClinicalAuthenticationGuard) @Controller('clinical/prescriptions')
export class PrescriptionController{
 constructor(private readonly s:PrescriptionService,private readonly c:ClinicalRequestContextService){}
 @Post() create(@Body() d:CreatePrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.create(d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/items') item(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AddMedicationDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addItem(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/safety-check') check(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:SafetyCheckDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.createSafetyCheck(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('safety-checks/:runId/complete') done(@Param('runId') id:string,@Body() d:any,@Req() r:any){return this.s.completeSafetyCheck(id,d.status,this.c.fromRequest(r),d);}
 @Post(':id/safety-alerts/:alertId/resolve') resolve(@Param('alertId') aid:string,@Body() d:ResolveSafetyAlertDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.resolveAlert(aid,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/sign') sign(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:SignPrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.sign(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/transmit') tx(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:TransmitPrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.transmit(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Get(':id/fulfillment') fulfill(@Param('id') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.fulfillment(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/cancel') cancel(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CancelPrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.cancel(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/fulfillments') fulfillmentRecord(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:PharmacyFulfillmentDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.recordFulfillment(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/receipt-events') receipt(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:MedicationReceiptDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.recordReceipt(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}

}

import { Body,Controller,Get,Param,Post,Req,UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClinicalAuthenticationGuard } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-authentication.guard';import { ClinicalRequestContextService } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-request-context.service';
import { PrescriptionService } from './prescription.service';import { AddMedicationDto,CreatePrescriptionDto,ResolveSafetyAlertDto,SafetyCheckDto,SignPrescriptionDto,TransmitPrescriptionDto,CancelPrescriptionDto,PharmacyFulfillmentDto,MedicationReceiptDto } from './prescription.dto';
@UseGuards(ClinicalAuthenticationGuard) @Controller('clinical/prescriptions')
export class PrescriptionController{
 constructor(private readonly s:PrescriptionService,private readonly c:ClinicalRequestContextService){}
 @Post() create(@Body() d:CreatePrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.create(d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/items') item(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AddMedicationDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addItem(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/safety-check') check(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:SafetyCheckDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.createSafetyCheck(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('safety-checks/:runId/complete') done(@Param('runId') id:string,@Body() d:any,@Req() r:any){return this.s.completeSafetyCheck(id,d.status,this.c.fromRequest(r),d);}
 @Post(':id/safety-alerts/:alertId/resolve') resolve(@Param('alertId') aid:string,@Body() d:ResolveSafetyAlertDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.resolveAlert(aid,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/sign') sign(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:SignPrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.sign(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/transmit') tx(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:TransmitPrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.transmit(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Get(':id/fulfillment') fulfill(@Param('id') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.fulfillment(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/cancel') cancel(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CancelPrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.cancel(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/fulfillments') fulfillmentRecord(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:PharmacyFulfillmentDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.recordFulfillment(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/receipt-events') receipt(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:MedicationReceiptDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.recordReceipt(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}

}

import { Body,Controller,Get,Param,Post,Req,UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClinicalAuthenticationGuard } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-authentication.guard';import { ClinicalRequestContextService } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-request-context.service';
import { PrescriptionService } from './prescription.service';import { AddMedicationDto,CreatePrescriptionDto,ResolveSafetyAlertDto,SafetyCheckDto,SignPrescriptionDto,TransmitPrescriptionDto,CancelPrescriptionDto,PharmacyFulfillmentDto,MedicationReceiptDto } from './prescription.dto';
@UseGuards(ClinicalAuthenticationGuard) @Controller('clinical/prescriptions')
export class PrescriptionController{
 constructor(private readonly s:PrescriptionService,private readonly c:ClinicalRequestContextService){}
 @Post() create(@Body() d:CreatePrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.create(d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/items') item(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AddMedicationDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.addItem(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/safety-check') check(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:SafetyCheckDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.createSafetyCheck(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('safety-checks/:runId/complete') done(@Param('runId') id:string,@Body() d:any,@Req() r:any){return this.s.completeSafetyCheck(id,d.status,this.c.fromRequest(r),d);}
 @Post(':id/safety-alerts/:alertId/resolve') resolve(@Param('alertId') aid:string,@Body() d:ResolveSafetyAlertDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.resolveAlert(aid,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/sign') sign(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:SignPrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.sign(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/transmit') tx(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:TransmitPrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.transmit(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Get(':id/fulfillment') fulfill(@Param('id') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.fulfillment(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/cancel') cancel(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CancelPrescriptionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.cancel(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/fulfillments') fulfillmentRecord(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:PharmacyFulfillmentDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.recordFulfillment(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post(':id/receipt-events') receipt(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:MedicationReceiptDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.recordReceipt(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}

}

import { IsIn,IsInt,IsOptional,IsString,IsUUID,Min } from '../../clinical-common/dto';
export class CreatePrescriptionDto{
 @IsUUID() careEncounterId!:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() remoteVisitSessionId?:string; @IsUUID() prescriberCapabilityGrantId!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() expiresAt?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() clinicalNoteRef?:string;
}
export class AddMedicationDto{
 @IsInt() @Min(1) sequenceNo!:number; @IsString() medicationCodeSystem!:string; @IsString() medicationCode!:string; @IsString() medicationDisplay!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() formulationCode?:string; @IsOptional() doseValue?:number; @IsOptional() @IsString() doseUnit?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() routeCode?:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() frequencyCode?:string; @IsOptional() @IsInt() @Min(1) durationDays?:number; @IsOptional() totalQuantity?:number;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() quantityUnit?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() administrationInstructionRef?:string;
}
export class SafetyCheckDto{
 @IsIn(['DUR','ALLERGY','DUPLICATION','INTERACTION','CONTRAINDICATION','DOSE_RANGE','PREGNANCY','RENAL_LIVER','OTHER']) checkType!:string;
 @IsIn(['EXTERNAL_DUR','INTERNAL_RULE_SUPPORT','PHARMACIST_REVIEW','CLINICIAN_REVIEW']) checkerType!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() checkerVersion?:string;
}
export class ResolveSafetyAlertDto{
 @IsIn(['ACCEPTED','OVERRIDDEN','RESOLVED','NOT_APPLICABLE']) resolutionStatus!:string; @IsString() resolutionReasonCode!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() resolutionRef?:string;
}
export class SignPrescriptionDto{ @IsUUID() signingCapabilityGrantId!:string; @IsString() fingerprintSha256!:string; @IsString() reasonCode!:string; @IsInt() @Min(1) expectedVersion!:number; }
export class TransmitPrescriptionDto{
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() destinationSystemId?:string; @IsIn(['EPRESCRIPTION_SYSTEM','PHARMACY','PATIENT','OTHER']) destinationType!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() destinationIdentifierRef?:string; @IsString() idempotencyKey!:string; @IsString() payloadRef!:string; @IsString() payloadSha256!:string;
}

export class CancelPrescriptionDto {
 @IsIn(['CANCELLED','VOID']) targetStatus!:string; @IsString() reasonCode!:string; @IsInt() @Min(1) expectedVersion!:number;
}
export class PharmacyFulfillmentDto {
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() pharmacyExternalSystemId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsString() pharmacyIdentifierRef?:string;
 @IsIn(['PENDING','ACCEPTED','PREPARING','PARTIALLY_DISPENSED','DISPENSED','DECLINED','CANCELLED']) fulfillmentStatus!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() externalFulfillmentRef?:string;
}
export class MedicationReceiptDto {
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() fulfillmentId?:string;
 @IsIn(['PATIENT_PICKUP','AUTHORIZED_PROXY_PICKUP','DELIVERY_CONFIRMED','OTHER']) receiptType!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() recipientReference?:string;
}

FILE: C:\realtime-server\PHASE2_COMPLETE_IMPLEMENTATION\PHASE2_COMPLETE_IMPLEMENTATION\src\clinical\prescription\prescription.service.ts
============================================================
import { ConflictException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { ClinicalRequestContext } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-types';
import { ClinicalCommandExecutor } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-command.executor';
import { ClinicalTableRepository } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-table.repository';
import { ClinicalSideEffectService } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-side-effect.service';
import { WorkforceCapabilityService } from '../../clinical-common/workforce-capability.service';
import { ClinicalReadExecutor } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-read.executor';

@Injectable()
export class PrescriptionService{
 constructor(private readonly tables:ClinicalTableRepository,private readonly commands:ClinicalCommandExecutor,private readonly sideEffects:ClinicalSideEffectService,private readonly capabilities:WorkforceCapabilityService,private readonly reads:ClinicalReadExecutor){}
 create(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_CREATE',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Prescriber workforce identity required');
    const encounter:any=await this.tables.findById('care_encounters',d.careEncounterId,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.prescriberCapabilityGrantId,ctx.workforceId,'CREATE_PRESCRIPTION',encounter.primary_site_id);
    return this.tables.insert('prescription_orders',{
      prescription_number:`RX-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,care_encounter_id:d.careEncounterId,remote_visit_session_id:d.remoteVisitSessionId,
      prescriber_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,prescriber_capability_grant_id:d.prescriberCapabilityGrantId,expires_at:d.expiresAt?new Date(d.expiresAt):undefined,
      clinical_note_ref:d.clinicalNoteRef,created_by_user_id:ctx.userId,
    },client);
   }});
 }
 addItem(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_ITEM_ADD',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client);
    if(rx.prescription_status!=='DRAFT') throw new ConflictException('Medication items editable only in DRAFT');
    return this.tables.insert('prescription_medication_items',{
      prescription_order_id:id,sequence_no:d.sequenceNo,medication_code_system:d.medicationCodeSystem,medication_code:d.medicationCode,
      medication_display:d.medicationDisplay,formulation_code:d.formulationCode,dose_value:d.doseValue,dose_unit:d.doseUnit,route_code:d.routeCode,
      frequency_code:d.frequencyCode,duration_days:d.durationDays,total_quantity:d.totalQuantity,quantity_unit:d.quantityUnit,
      administration_instruction_ref:d.administrationInstructionRef,item_status:'ACTIVE',
    },client);
   }});
 }
 createSafetyCheck(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_SAFETY_CHECK_CREATE',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client,true);
    if(['SIGNED','TRANSMITTED','FULFILLED','CANCELLED','VOID'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Prescription is not safety-checkable');
    await this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',id,{prescription_status:'SAFETY_CHECK_PENDING',version:Number(rx.version)+1},client);
    const run:any=await this.tables.insert('medication_safety_check_runs',{
      prescription_order_id:id,check_type:d.checkType,checker_type:d.checkerType,checker_version:d.checkerVersion,check_status:'PENDING',
    },client);
    if(d.checkerType==='EXTERNAL_DUR'){
      await this.sideEffects.enqueue(client,{
        jobType:'DUR_CHECK',aggregateType:'MEDICATION_SAFETY_CHECK',aggregateId:run.id,
        idempotencyKey:`dur-check:${run.id}`,payload:{safetyCheckRunId:run.id,prescriptionId:id},createdByUserId:ctx.userId,
      });
    }
    return run;
   }});
 }
 completeSafetyCheck(runId:string,status:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext,refs:any={}){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_SAFETY_CHECK_COMPLETE',resourceType:'SAFETY_CHECK',resourceId:runId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const run:any=await this.tables.findById('medication_safety_check_runs',runId,client,true);
    const updated=await this.tables.updateById('medication_safety_check_runs',runId,{
      check_status:status,request_ref:refs.requestRef,response_ref:refs.responseRef,completed_at:new Date(),
    },client);
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',run.prescription_order_id,client,true);
    const alerts:any[]=await client.query(`SELECT a.* FROM medication_safety_alerts a JOIN medication_safety_check_runs r ON r.id=a.safety_check_run_id WHERE r.prescription_order_id=$1 AND a.resolution_status='OPEN'`,[rx.id]).then(x=>x.rows);
    const blocking=alerts.some(a=>a.alert_severity==='BLOCKING');
    await this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',rx.id,{prescription_status:blocking?'SAFETY_REVIEW_REQUIRED':'READY_TO_SIGN',version:Number(rx.version)+1},client);
    return updated;
   }});
 }
 resolveAlert(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_ALERT_RESOLVE',resourceType:'MEDICATION_SAFETY_ALERT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    return this.tables.updateById('medication_safety_alerts',id,{
      resolution_status:d.resolutionStatus,resolved_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,resolution_reason_code:d.resolutionReasonCode,
      resolution_ref:d.resolutionRef,resolved_at:new Date(),
    },client);
   }});
 }
 sign(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIPTION_SIGN',action:'PRESCRIPTION_SIGN',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Prescriber workforce identity required');
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client,true);
    if(rx.prescriber_workforce_id!==ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Only prescriber can sign');
    const encounter:any=await this.tables.findById('care_encounters',rx.care_encounter_id,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.signingCapabilityGrantId,ctx.workforceId,'SIGN_PRESCRIPTION',encounter.primary_site_id);
    if(rx.prescription_status!=='READY_TO_SIGN') throw new ConflictException('Prescription must be READY_TO_SIGN');
    const blocking=await client.query(`SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS n FROM medication_safety_alerts a JOIN medication_safety_check_runs r ON r.id=a.safety_check_run_id WHERE r.prescription_order_id=$1 AND a.resolution_status='OPEN' AND a.alert_severity='BLOCKING'`,[id]);
    if(Number(blocking.rows[0]?.n)>0) throw new ConflictException('Blocking medication safety alerts remain open');
    const updated=await this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',id,{
      prescription_status:'SIGNED',issued_at:new Date(),signed_at:new Date(),version:Number(rx.version)+1,
    },client,d.expectedVersion);
    await this.tables.insert('prescription_signing_events',{
      prescription_order_id:id,actor_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,
      event_type:'SIGNED',prescription_fingerprint_sha256:d.fingerprintSha256,reason_code:d.reasonCode,
    },client);
    return updated;
   }});
 }
 transmit(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_TRANSMIT_QUEUE',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client,true);
    if(!['SIGNED','TRANSMISSION_PENDING','TRANSMITTED'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Prescription is not transmittable');
    const tx:any=await this.tables.insert('prescription_transmissions',{
      prescription_order_id:id,destination_system_id:d.destinationSystemId,destination_type:d.destinationType,
      destination_identifier_ref:d.destinationIdentifierRef,idempotency_key:d.idempotencyKey,payload_ref:d.payloadRef,payload_sha256:d.payloadSha256,
    },client);
    if(rx.prescription_status==='SIGNED') await this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',id,{prescription_status:'TRANSMISSION_PENDING',version:Number(rx.version)+1},client);
    await this.sideEffects.enqueue(client,{
      jobType:'PRESCRIPTION_TRANSMIT',aggregateType:'PRESCRIPTION_TRANSMISSION',aggregateId:tx.id,
      idempotencyKey:`prescription-transmit:${d.idempotencyKey}`,payload:{transmissionId:tx.id},createdByUserId:ctx.userId,
    });
    return tx;
   }});
 }
 fulfillment(id:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.reads.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_FULFILLMENT_READ',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,handler:client=>this.tables.list('pharmacy_fulfillments',{prescription_order_id:id},100,client)});}
 cancel(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:`PRESCRIPTION_${d.targetStatus}`,resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client,true);
    if(['FULFILLED','CANCELLED','VOID'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Prescription is terminal');
    return this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',id,{
      prescription_status:d.targetStatus,cancellation_reason_code:d.reasonCode,version:Number(rx.version)+1,
    },client,d.expectedVersion);
   }});
 }

 recordFulfillment(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PHARMACY_FULFILLMENT_RECORD',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client,true);
    if(!['TRANSMITTED','PARTIALLY_FULFILLED'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Prescription must be transmitted before fulfillment');
    const row:any=await this.tables.insert('pharmacy_fulfillments',{
      prescription_order_id:id,pharmacy_external_system_id:d.pharmacyExternalSystemId,pharmacy_identifier_ref:d.pharmacyIdentifierRef,
      fulfillment_status:d.fulfillmentStatus,accepted_at:['ACCEPTED','PREPARING','PARTIALLY_DISPENSED','DISPENSED'].includes(d.fulfillmentStatus)?new Date():undefined,
      dispensed_at:d.fulfillmentStatus==='DISPENSED'?new Date():undefined,external_fulfillment_ref:d.externalFulfillmentRef,
    },client);
    const next=d.fulfillmentStatus==='DISPENSED'?'FULFILLED':d.fulfillmentStatus==='PARTIALLY_DISPENSED'?'PARTIALLY_FULFILLED':rx.prescription_status;
    if(next!==rx.prescription_status) await this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',id,{prescription_status:next,version:Number(rx.version)+1},client);
    return row;
   }});
 }

 recordReceipt(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'MEDICATION_RECEIPT_RECORD',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client);
    if(!['PARTIALLY_FULFILLED','FULFILLED'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Medication cannot be received before fulfillment');
    if(d.fulfillmentId){
      const f:any=await this.tables.findById('pharmacy_fulfillments',d.fulfillmentId,client);
      if(f.prescription_order_id!==id) throw new ConflictException('Fulfillment prescription mismatch');
    }
    return this.tables.insert('medication_receipt_events',{
      prescription_order_id:id,fulfillment_id:d.fulfillmentId,receipt_type:d.receiptType,
      recipient_reference:d.recipientReference,confirmed_by_user_id:ctx.userId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

}

FILE: C:\realtime-server\SERVER_PHASE3_COMPLETE\SERVER_PHASE3_COMPLETE\payload\phase2\src\clinical\prescription\prescription.service.ts
============================================================
import { ConflictException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { ClinicalRequestContext } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-types';
import { ClinicalCommandExecutor } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-command.executor';
import { ClinicalTableRepository } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-table.repository';
import { ClinicalSideEffectService } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-side-effect.service';
import { WorkforceCapabilityService } from '../../clinical-common/workforce-capability.service';
import { ClinicalReadExecutor } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-read.executor';

@Injectable()
export class PrescriptionService{
 constructor(private readonly tables:ClinicalTableRepository,private readonly commands:ClinicalCommandExecutor,private readonly sideEffects:ClinicalSideEffectService,private readonly capabilities:WorkforceCapabilityService,private readonly reads:ClinicalReadExecutor){}
 create(d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_CREATE',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Prescriber workforce identity required');
    const encounter:any=await this.tables.findById('care_encounters',d.careEncounterId,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.prescriberCapabilityGrantId,ctx.workforceId,'CREATE_PRESCRIPTION',encounter.primary_site_id);
    return this.tables.insert('prescription_orders',{
      prescription_number:`RX-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,care_encounter_id:d.careEncounterId,remote_visit_session_id:d.remoteVisitSessionId,
      prescriber_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,prescriber_capability_grant_id:d.prescriberCapabilityGrantId,expires_at:d.expiresAt?new Date(d.expiresAt):undefined,
      clinical_note_ref:d.clinicalNoteRef,created_by_user_id:ctx.userId,
    },client);
   }});
 }
 addItem(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_ITEM_ADD',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client);
    if(rx.prescription_status!=='DRAFT') throw new ConflictException('Medication items editable only in DRAFT');
    return this.tables.insert('prescription_medication_items',{
      prescription_order_id:id,sequence_no:d.sequenceNo,medication_code_system:d.medicationCodeSystem,medication_code:d.medicationCode,
      medication_display:d.medicationDisplay,formulation_code:d.formulationCode,dose_value:d.doseValue,dose_unit:d.doseUnit,route_code:d.routeCode,
      frequency_code:d.frequencyCode,duration_days:d.durationDays,total_quantity:d.totalQuantity,quantity_unit:d.quantityUnit,
      administration_instruction_ref:d.administrationInstructionRef,item_status:'ACTIVE',
    },client);
   }});
 }
 createSafetyCheck(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_SAFETY_CHECK_CREATE',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client,true);
    if(['SIGNED','TRANSMITTED','FULFILLED','CANCELLED','VOID'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Prescription is not safety-checkable');
    await this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',id,{prescription_status:'SAFETY_CHECK_PENDING',version:Number(rx.version)+1},client);
    const run:any=await this.tables.insert('medication_safety_check_runs',{
      prescription_order_id:id,check_type:d.checkType,checker_type:d.checkerType,checker_version:d.checkerVersion,check_status:'PENDING',
    },client);
    if(d.checkerType==='EXTERNAL_DUR'){
      await this.sideEffects.enqueue(client,{
        jobType:'DUR_CHECK',aggregateType:'MEDICATION_SAFETY_CHECK',aggregateId:run.id,
        idempotencyKey:`dur-check:${run.id}`,payload:{safetyCheckRunId:run.id,prescriptionId:id},createdByUserId:ctx.userId,
      });
    }
    return run;
   }});
 }
 completeSafetyCheck(runId:string,status:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext,refs:any={}){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_SAFETY_CHECK_COMPLETE',resourceType:'SAFETY_CHECK',resourceId:runId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const run:any=await this.tables.findById('medication_safety_check_runs',runId,client,true);
    const updated=await this.tables.updateById('medication_safety_check_runs',runId,{
      check_status:status,request_ref:refs.requestRef,response_ref:refs.responseRef,completed_at:new Date(),
    },client);
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',run.prescription_order_id,client,true);
    const alerts:any[]=await client.query(`SELECT a.* FROM medication_safety_alerts a JOIN medication_safety_check_runs r ON r.id=a.safety_check_run_id WHERE r.prescription_order_id=$1 AND a.resolution_status='OPEN'`,[rx.id]).then(x=>x.rows);
    const blocking=alerts.some(a=>a.alert_severity==='BLOCKING');
    await this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',rx.id,{prescription_status:blocking?'SAFETY_REVIEW_REQUIRED':'READY_TO_SIGN',version:Number(rx.version)+1},client);
    return updated;
   }});
 }
 resolveAlert(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_ALERT_RESOLVE',resourceType:'MEDICATION_SAFETY_ALERT',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    return this.tables.updateById('medication_safety_alerts',id,{
      resolution_status:d.resolutionStatus,resolved_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,resolution_reason_code:d.resolutionReasonCode,
      resolution_ref:d.resolutionRef,resolved_at:new Date(),
    },client);
   }});
 }
 sign(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIPTION_SIGN',action:'PRESCRIPTION_SIGN',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Prescriber workforce identity required');
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client,true);
    if(rx.prescriber_workforce_id!==ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Only prescriber can sign');
    const encounter:any=await this.tables.findById('care_encounters',rx.care_encounter_id,client);
    await this.capabilities.assertGrant(client,d.signingCapabilityGrantId,ctx.workforceId,'SIGN_PRESCRIPTION',encounter.primary_site_id);
    if(rx.prescription_status!=='READY_TO_SIGN') throw new ConflictException('Prescription must be READY_TO_SIGN');
    const blocking=await client.query(`SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS n FROM medication_safety_alerts a JOIN medication_safety_check_runs r ON r.id=a.safety_check_run_id WHERE r.prescription_order_id=$1 AND a.resolution_status='OPEN' AND a.alert_severity='BLOCKING'`,[id]);
    if(Number(blocking.rows[0]?.n)>0) throw new ConflictException('Blocking medication safety alerts remain open');
    const updated=await this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',id,{
      prescription_status:'SIGNED',issued_at:new Date(),signed_at:new Date(),version:Number(rx.version)+1,
    },client,d.expectedVersion);
    await this.tables.insert('prescription_signing_events',{
      prescription_order_id:id,actor_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,capability_grant_id:d.signingCapabilityGrantId,
      event_type:'SIGNED',prescription_fingerprint_sha256:d.fingerprintSha256,reason_code:d.reasonCode,
    },client);
    return updated;
   }});
 }
 transmit(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_TRANSMIT_QUEUE',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client,true);
    if(!['SIGNED','TRANSMISSION_PENDING','TRANSMITTED'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Prescription is not transmittable');
    const tx:any=await this.tables.insert('prescription_transmissions',{
      prescription_order_id:id,destination_system_id:d.destinationSystemId,destination_type:d.destinationType,
      destination_identifier_ref:d.destinationIdentifierRef,idempotency_key:d.idempotencyKey,payload_ref:d.payloadRef,payload_sha256:d.payloadSha256,
    },client);
    if(rx.prescription_status==='SIGNED') await this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',id,{prescription_status:'TRANSMISSION_PENDING',version:Number(rx.version)+1},client);
    await this.sideEffects.enqueue(client,{
      jobType:'PRESCRIPTION_TRANSMIT',aggregateType:'PRESCRIPTION_TRANSMISSION',aggregateId:tx.id,
      idempotencyKey:`prescription-transmit:${d.idempotencyKey}`,payload:{transmissionId:tx.id},createdByUserId:ctx.userId,
    });
    return tx;
   }});
 }
 fulfillment(id:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.reads.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PRESCRIPTION_FULFILLMENT_READ',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,handler:client=>this.tables.list('pharmacy_fulfillments',{prescription_order_id:id},100,client)});}
 cancel(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:`PRESCRIPTION_${d.targetStatus}`,resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client,true);
    if(['FULFILLED','CANCELLED','VOID'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Prescription is terminal');
    return this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',id,{
      prescription_status:d.targetStatus,cancellation_reason_code:d.reasonCode,version:Number(rx.version)+1,
    },client,d.expectedVersion);
   }});
 }

 recordFulfillment(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'PHARMACY_FULFILLMENT_RECORD',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client,true);
    if(!['TRANSMITTED','PARTIALLY_FULFILLED'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Prescription must be transmitted before fulfillment');
    const row:any=await this.tables.insert('pharmacy_fulfillments',{
      prescription_order_id:id,pharmacy_external_system_id:d.pharmacyExternalSystemId,pharmacy_identifier_ref:d.pharmacyIdentifierRef,
      fulfillment_status:d.fulfillmentStatus,accepted_at:['ACCEPTED','PREPARING','PARTIALLY_DISPENSED','DISPENSED'].includes(d.fulfillmentStatus)?new Date():undefined,
      dispensed_at:d.fulfillmentStatus==='DISPENSED'?new Date():undefined,external_fulfillment_ref:d.externalFulfillmentRef,
    },client);
    const next=d.fulfillmentStatus==='DISPENSED'?'FULFILLED':d.fulfillmentStatus==='PARTIALLY_DISPENSED'?'PARTIALLY_FULFILLED':rx.prescription_status;
    if(next!==rx.prescription_status) await this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',id,{prescription_status:next,version:Number(rx.version)+1},client);
    return row;
   }});
 }

 recordReceipt(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'MEDICATION_RECEIPT_RECORD',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client);
    if(!['PARTIALLY_FULFILLED','FULFILLED'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Medication cannot be received before fulfillment');
    if(d.fulfillmentId){
      const f:any=await this.tables.findById('pharmacy_fulfillments',d.fulfillmentId,client);
      if(f.prescription_order_id!==id) throw new ConflictException('Fulfillment prescription mismatch');
    }
    return this.tables.insert('medication_receipt_events',{
      prescription_order_id:id,fulfillment_id:d.fulfillmentId,receipt_type:d.receiptType,
      recipient_reference:d.recipientReference,confirmed_by_user_id:ctx.userId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

}

    if(!['TRANSMITTED','PARTIALLY_FULFILLED'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Prescription must be transmitted before fulfillment');
    const row:any=await this.tables.insert('pharmacy_fulfillments',{
      prescription_order_id:id,pharmacy_external_system_id:d.pharmacyExternalSystemId,pharmacy_identifier_ref:d.pharmacyIdentifierRef,
      fulfillment_status:d.fulfillmentStatus,accepted_at:['ACCEPTED','PREPARING','PARTIALLY_DISPENSED','DISPENSED'].includes(d.fulfillmentStatus)?new Date():undefined,
      dispensed_at:d.fulfillmentStatus==='DISPENSED'?new Date():undefined,external_fulfillment_ref:d.externalFulfillmentRef,
    },client);
    const next=d.fulfillmentStatus==='DISPENSED'?'FULFILLED':d.fulfillmentStatus==='PARTIALLY_DISPENSED'?'PARTIALLY_FULFILLED':rx.prescription_status;
    if(next!==rx.prescription_status) await this.tables.updateById('prescription_orders',id,{prescription_status:next,version:Number(rx.version)+1},client);
    return row;
   }});
 }

 recordReceipt(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_PRESCRIBE',action:'MEDICATION_RECEIPT_RECORD',resourceType:'PRESCRIPTION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const rx:any=await this.tables.findById('prescription_orders',id,client);
    if(!['PARTIALLY_FULFILLED','FULFILLED'].includes(rx.prescription_status)) throw new ConflictException('Medication cannot be received before fulfillment');
    if(d.fulfillmentId){
      const f:any=await this.tables.findById('pharmacy_fulfillments',d.fulfillmentId,client);
      if(f.prescription_order_id!==id) throw new ConflictException('Fulfillment prescription mismatch');
    }
    return this.tables.insert('medication_receipt_events',{
      prescription_order_id:id,fulfillment_id:d.fulfillmentId,receipt_type:d.receiptType,
      recipient_reference:d.recipientReference,confirmed_by_user_id:ctx.userId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

}

import {
  IsBoolean, IsIn, IsInt, IsNumber, IsObject, IsOptional, IsString, IsUUID, Min,
} from '../../clinical-common/dto';

export class CreateAppointmentSlotDto {
  @IsUUID() siteId!: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsUUID() clinicianWorkforceId?: string;
  @IsIn(['FIRST_VISIT','FOLLOW_UP','REVIEW','OTHER']) visitType!: string;
  @IsIn(['VIDEO','AUDIO_EXCEPTION','IN_PERSON_HANDOFF']) modality!: string;
  @IsString() slotStartAt!: string;
  @IsString() slotEndAt!: string;
  @IsInt() @Min(1) capacity!: number;
  @IsOptional() @IsObject() accessibilitySupport?: Record<string,unknown>;
}

export class BookAppointmentDto {
  @IsString() patientUserId!: string;
  @IsUUID() siteId!: string;
  @IsUUID() appointmentSlotId!: string;
  @IsIn(['PLATFORM','PHONE','GUARDIAN','PUBLIC_SERVICE','STAFF']) bookingChannel!: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsUUID() supportRelationId?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() patientInstructionRef?: string;
}

export class AppointmentStatusDto {
  @IsIn(['ARRIVED','IN_SERVICE','COMPLETED','CANCELLED','NO_SHOW']) status!: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() reasonCode?: string;
  @IsInt() @Min(1) expectedVersion!: number;
  @IsOptional() @IsUUID() careEncounterId?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsUUID() remoteVisitSessionId?: string;
}

export class CreateSupportRelationDto {
  @IsString() supporterUserId!: string;
  @IsIn(['LEGAL_GUARDIAN','FAMILY','CAREGIVER','ACTIVITY_ASSISTANT','PUBLIC_HEALTH_STAFF','INTERPRETER','OTHER']) relationType!: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsObject() scope?: Record<string,unknown>;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() verificationRef?: string;
}

export class VerifySupportRelationDto {
  @IsString() verificationRef!: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() expiresAt?: string;
}

export class RevokeSupportRelationDto {
  @IsString() reasonRef!: string;
}

export class HealthInformationSyncDto {
  @IsIn(['HIRA','MYHEALTHWAY','HOSPITAL','FHIR','OTHER']) sourceSystem!: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsUUID() careEncounterId?: string;
  @IsObject() requestedScope!: Record<string,unknown>;
}

  @IsBoolean() medicationFollowupRequired!: boolean;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() escalationInstructionRef?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() planRef?: string;
  @IsOptional() items?: Array<{
    itemType: 'CHECKIN'|'MEASUREMENT'|'MEDICATION'|'EDUCATION'|'APPOINTMENT';
    itemCode: string;
    schedule?: Record<string,unknown>;
    instructionRef?: string;
  }>;
}

export class FollowUpPlanStatusDto {

  @IsInt() @Min(1) expectedVersion!: number;
}

export class FollowUpCheckinDto {
  @IsIn(['PATIENT','GUARDIAN','CAREGIVER','STAFF']) sourceRole!: string;

  @IsIn(['IMPROVING','STABLE','WORSENING','URGENT_REVIEW']) overallStatus!: string;
}

export class FollowUpMeasurementDto {
  @IsString() measurementType!: string;

  @IsString() measuredAt!: string;
}

export class AdverseEventDto {

  @IsString() eventRef!: string;
}

export class AdverseEventActionDto {
  @IsIn(['ACKNOWLEDGED','ESCALATED_IN_PERSON','ESCALATED_EMERGENCY','RESOLVED']) status!: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() resolutionRef?: string;
}

export class CreatePatientNotificationDto {
  @IsOptional() @IsUUID() careEncounterId?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsUUID() followUpPlanId?: string;
  @IsIn(['APPOINTMENT','CHECKIN_DUE','MEASUREMENT_DUE','MEDICATION','RESULT_AVAILABLE','FOLLOW_UP','SAFETY','SYSTEM']) notificationType!: string;
  @IsIn(['IN_APP','SMS','PUSH','EMAIL']) channel!: string;
  @IsString() templateCode!: string;
  @IsString() contentRef!: string;
  @IsString() idempotencyKey!: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() scheduledAt?: string;
}

export class UpsertPharmacyDto {
  @IsString() pharmacyCode!: string;
  @IsString() displayName!: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() regionCode?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() addressRef?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsString() phoneRef?: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsNumber() latitude?: number;
  @IsOptional() @IsNumber() longitude?: number;
  @IsOptional() @IsObject() capabilities?: Record<string,unknown>;
  @IsOptional() @IsUUID() externalSystemId?: string;
}

export class SelectPharmacyDto {
  @IsUUID() pharmacyDirectoryEntryId!: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsUUID() supportRelationId?: string;
}

export class PatientVisitPrecheckDto {
  @IsIn(['PASS','FAIL','NOT_REQUIRED','UNKNOWN']) cameraState!: string;
  @IsIn(['PASS','FAIL','NOT_REQUIRED','UNKNOWN']) microphoneState!: string;
  @IsIn(['PASS','FAIL','NOT_REQUIRED','UNKNOWN']) speakerState!: string;
  @IsIn(['PASS','WARNING','FAIL','UNKNOWN']) networkState!: string;
  @IsOptional() @IsInt() @Min(0) measuredRttMs?: number;
  @IsOptional() @IsInt() @Min(0) measuredDownlinkKbps?: number;
  @IsOptional() @IsInt() @Min(0) measuredUplinkKbps?: number;
  @IsOptional() @IsObject() checkPayload?: Record<string,unknown>;
}

export class CancelAppointmentSelfDto {
  @IsInt() @Min(1) expectedVersion!: number;
  @IsString() reasonCode!: string;
}
